# What lift() leaves alone against iPhoneOS16.4.sdk (the archive the iphoneos-sdk package installs for 16.4): one full lift of the registry of this series
# (main 427544e31 with stack 16: the rows no SDK header declares - Metal's reflection and conversions, SensorKit, HealthKit r4, SwiftData, MapKit's map item, the tagged buffers, UIKit 7-12, PHASE and Security), none dropped: 1081 lines.
# Each is a name the lift did not reach, and each was looked for in every header of the SDK (usr/include and the frameworks) and found declared nowhere,
# but for the ones told here:
#  - class (82): a class no header declares (a stub exports some, some are private, some a later SDK's).
#  - undeclared (553): a registered member its class reaches no declaration of; the line says where the SDK declares it, or that it declares it nowhere.
#  - unmatched (446): __sincosf_stret and __sincos_stret are declared by math.h, in the branch of __IPHONE_OS_VERSION_MIN_REQUIRED >= 70000, which the 6.1.3 target does not read.
#    The rest (CG*(), NSData*, NSExtensionMain, BLAS{Get,Set}Threading(), __NS*, __kCF*, compiler-rt and libSystem internals) are exported by a stub or private, or arrived after this SDK, and named by no header.
